    <script>
    document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {
        Alpine.data('voiceSearch', () => ({
            searchText: '',
            recognition: null,
            isRecording: false,
            timeout: null,

            init() {
                if (!('webkitSpeechRecognition' in window)) {
                    console.warn('Tarayıcınız sesli aramayı desteklemiyor.');
                    return;
                }

                this.recognition = new webkitSpeechRecognition();
                this.recognition.lang = 'tr';
                this.recognition.interimResults = false;
                this.recognition.maxAlternatives = 1;

                this.recognition.onstart = () => {
                    this.isRecording = true;
                    this.timeout = setTimeout(() => {
                        if (this.isRecording) {
                            this.recognition.stop();
                        }
                    }, 15000);
                };

                this.recognition.onend = () => {
                    this.isRecording = false;
                    clearTimeout(this.timeout);

                };

                this.recognition.onresult = (event) => {
                    const transcript = event.results[0][0].transcript;
                    this.searchText = transcript;
                    const input = document.getElementById('q');
                    if (input) {
                        input.value = transcript;
                        input.focus();
                    }

                    const form = (input && input.closest('form')) || document.querySelector('form[action="https://www.karamangundem.com/arama"]');
                    if (form && transcript.length >= 3) {
                        setTimeout(() => form.submit(), 500);
                    }
                };

                this.recognition.onerror = (event) => {
                    this.isRecording = false;
                    clearTimeout(this.timeout);
                    console.error('Ses tanıma hatası:', event.error);
                };
            },

            startVoiceSearch() {
                if (!this.recognition) {
                    return;
                }
                if (this.isRecording) {
                    this.recognition.stop();
                    return;
                }

                this.recognition.start();
            }
        }));
    });
</script>

                        init() {
                            this.startTicker();
                        },

                        startTicker() {
                            this.interval = setInterval(() => {
                                this.next();
                            }, 4000);
                        },

                        stopTicker() {
                            clearInterval(this.interval);
                        },

                        next() {
                            if (this.currentIndex < this.newsItems.length - 4) {
                                this.currentIndex++;
                            } else {
                                this.currentIndex = 0;
                            }
                        },

                        prev() {
                            if (this.currentIndex > 0) {
                                this.currentIndex--;
                            } else {
                                this.currentIndex = this.newsItems.length - 4;
                            }
                        },

                        truncateTitle(title, maxLength) {
                            return title.length > maxLength ? title.substring(0, maxLength) + '...' : title;
                        }
                    }
                }

<style>.logo-koyu { filter: brightness(0) !important; } .logo-beyaz { filter: brightness(0) invert(1) !important; }</style>
<script>
    (function () {
        const logos = {
            desktop: {
                light: 'https://www.karamangundem.com/images/generals/logo.png?v=2898',
                dark: 'https://www.karamangundem.com/images/generals/logo-night.png?v=2898',
            },
            mobile: {
                light: 'https://www.karamangundem.com/images/generals/mobil-logo.png?v=2898',
                dark: 'https://www.karamangundem.com/images/generals/mobil-logo-night.png?v=2898',
            }
        };
        const logoSizes = {"desktopLight":[160,76],"desktopDark":[160,76],"mobileLight":[160,76],"mobileDark":[160,76]};
        const lightLogo = {"desktop":false,"mobile":false};
        const logoTones = {"desktop":[[255,255,255,0.306],[170,34,34,0.158],[34,34,34,0.046],[119,34,34,0.042],[238,238,238,0.037],[238,221,221,0.028],[170,51,51,0.023],[187,85,85,0.022]],"mobile":[[255,255,255,0.306],[170,34,34,0.158],[34,34,34,0.046],[119,34,34,0.042],[238,238,238,0.037],[238,221,221,0.028],[170,51,51,0.023],[187,85,85,0.022]]};

        function bgColor(el) {
            for (let p = el.parentElement; p; p = p.parentElement) {
                const m = getComputedStyle(p).backgroundColor.match(/[\d.]+/g);
                if (m && m.length >= 3 && !(m.length > 3 && +m[3] === 0)) {
                    return [+m[0], +m[1], +m[2]];
                }
            }
            return [255, 255, 255];
        }

        function bgLight(el) {
            const c = bgColor(el);
            return (0.2126 * c[0] + 0.7152 * c[1] + 0.0722 * c[2]) / 255 > 0.6;
        }

        function lum(c) {
            const f = v => (v /= 255) <= 0.03928 ? v / 12.92 : Math.pow((v + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
            return 0.2126 * f(c[0]) + 0.7152 * f(c[1]) + 0.0722 * f(c[2]);
        }

        function shares(el, tones) {
            const bl = lum(bgColor(el));
            let blend = 0, visible = 0;
            (tones || []).forEach(t => {
                const l = lum(t), c = (Math.max(l, bl) + 0.05) / (Math.min(l, bl) + 0.05);
                if (c < 1.6) blend += t[3];
                if (c >= 2.5) visible += t[3];
            });
            return { blend: blend, visible: visible };
        }

        function blends(el, tones) {
            if (!tones || !tones.length) return false;
            const s = shares(el, tones);
            return s.blend >= 0.6 && s.visible < 0.1;
        }

        function updateLogos() {
            let darkMode = false;
            try { darkMode = localStorage.getItem('darkMode') === 'true'; } catch (e) {}
            const isMobile = window.innerWidth < 768;

            document.documentElement.classList.toggle('dark', darkMode);

            const key = (isMobile ? 'mobile' : 'desktop') + (darkMode ? 'Dark' : 'Light');
            const src = isMobile
                ? (darkMode ? logos.mobile.dark : logos.mobile.light)
                : (darkMode ? logos.desktop.dark : logos.desktop.light);
            const size = logoSizes[key];

            ['siteLogo', 'siteLogoTop'].forEach(id => {
                const el = document.getElementById(id);
                if (el && el.src !== src) {
                    if (size) {
                        el.setAttribute('width', size[0]);
                        el.setAttribute('height', size[1]);
                    } else if (el.getAttribute('width') !== '100%') {
                        el.setAttribute('width', '100%');
                        el.setAttribute('height', '100%');
                    }
                    el.src = src;
                }
                if (el) {
                    const dev = isMobile ? 'mobile' : 'desktop';
                    const light = !darkMode && lightLogo[dev] && bgLight(el) && shares(el, logoTones[dev]).visible < 0.1;
                    const blend = !darkMode && !light && blends(el, logoTones[dev]);
                    el.classList.toggle('logo-koyu', light || (blend && bgLight(el)));
                    el.classList.toggle('logo-beyaz', blend && !bgLight(el));
                }
            });
        }

        updateLogos();
        if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', updateLogos);

        window.addEventListener('resize', updateLogos);

        window.addEventListener('storage', e => {
            if (e.key === 'darkMode') updateLogos();
        });

        try {
            const _setItem = localStorage.setItem;
            localStorage.setItem = function (k, v) {
                _setItem.apply(this, arguments);
                if (k === 'darkMode') updateLogos();
            };
        } catch (e) {}
    })();
</script>

        <script>
            function voiceComponent(postId) {
                return {
                    isSpeaking: false,
                    startSpeaking() {
                        if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
                            var m1 = 'Dinle: Tarayıcınız sesli okumayı desteklemiyor.';
                            window.Swal ? Swal.fire({icon: 'info', text: m1}) : alert(m1);
                            return;
                        }

                        var postElement = document.getElementById('post-' + postId);
                        if (!postElement) return;

                        var postTitleElement = postElement.querySelector('.post-title');
                        var postBodyElement = postElement.querySelector('.post-body');

                        if (!postTitleElement || !postBodyElement) return;

                        var postTitle = postTitleElement.textContent.trim();
                        var postBody = postBodyElement.innerText.trim();

                        var textToSpeak = postTitle + ". " + postBody;

                        
                        window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

                        var self = this;

                        
                        var konus = function () {
                            
                            var dil = 'tr';
                            var sesler = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices() || [];
                            var kod = dil.split('-')[0].toLowerCase();
                            var uygun = sesler.filter(function (v) {
                                return (v.lang || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(kod) === 0;
                            });

                            if (!uygun.length && sesler.length) {
                                var m2 = 'Cihazınızda bu dil için ses paketi bulunamadı.';
                                window.Swal ? Swal.fire({icon: 'info', text: m2}) : alert(m2);
                                return;
                            }

                            
                            var parcalar = (textToSpeak.match(/[^.!?…]+[.!?…]*\s*/g) || [textToSpeak])
                                .reduce(function (yig, c) {
                                    var son = yig[yig.length - 1];
                                    if (son && (son + c).length < 180) { yig[yig.length - 1] = son + c; }
                                    else { yig.push(c); }
                                    return yig;
                                }, [])
                                .filter(function (p) { return p.trim().length; });

                            var sira = 0;
                            var sonraki = function () {
                                if (sira >= parcalar.length) { self.isSpeaking = false; return; }

                                var u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(parcalar[sira++]);
                                u.lang = dil;
                                u.rate = 1;
                                if (uygun.length) { u.voice = uygun[0]; }

                                u.onstart = function () { self.isSpeaking = true; };
                                u.onend = function () { if (self.isSpeaking) { sonraki(); } };
                                u.onerror = function () { self.isSpeaking = false; };

                                window.speechSynthesis.speak(u);
                            };

                            sonraki();
                        };

                        if ((window.speechSynthesis.getVoices() || []).length === 0) {
                            window.speechSynthesis.addEventListener('voiceschanged', function h() {
                                window.speechSynthesis.removeEventListener('voiceschanged', h);
                                konus();
                            });
                            
                            setTimeout(function () { if (!self.isSpeaking) { konus(); } }, 1200);
                        } else {
                            konus();
                        }
                    },
                    stopSpeaking() {
                        if ('speechSynthesis' in window) {
                            window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
                        }
                        this.isSpeaking = false;
                    }
                }
            }

            
            window.addEventListener('beforeunload', function () {
                if ('speechSynthesis' in window) { window.speechSynthesis.cancel(); }
            });
        </script>

                                                                 property="articleBody"><p>YENİ Parti'de genel başkanlık ön seçimi heyecanı Karaman'da da yaşanıyor. Karaman Milletvekili İsmail Atakan Ünver ile İl Başkanı Ahmet Recai Evcen, 8 Kasım Pazar günü İl Başkanlığında kurulan sandıkta oylarını kullandı. Her iki isim de üyelerin karar süreçlerine doğrudan katılımına dikkat çekti.</p>

<p><img alt="" class="image-align-left" height="1152" src="https://images.karamangundem.com/uploads/2026/11/897607948-10164855679283604-6667397100990745529-n-6af04efcf067b.jpg" width="400" />Türkiye genelinde 81 ilde yaklaşık 3 bin sandıkta gerçekleştirilen ön seçimde mevcut Genel Başkan Özgür Özel tek aday olarak yer alıyor. Oy verme işlemleri saat 09.00'da başlarken sandıkların 17.00'de kapanması planlanıyor. </p>

<h2><span style="color:#e74c3c;"><span style="font-size:20px;">ÜNVER: “VATANDAŞIN VE ÜYENİN SESİNİN DUYULDUĞU BİR SİYASET”</h2>

<p>Karaman İl Başkanlığında oyunu kullanan Milletvekili İsmail Atakan Ünver, parti yönetiminde üyelerin söz sahibi olmasının önemini vurguladı.</p>

<p>Ünver, açıklamasında şu ifadeleri kullandı:<img alt="" class="image-align-right" height="200" src="https://images.karamangundem.com/uploads/2026/11/889001887-18651202369041492-9019578133255222223-n-6af04f1e1fc86.jpg" width="150" /></p>

<p>“Genel Başkanlık Önseçimi için İl Başkanlığımızda oyumuzu kullandık. YENİ Parti olarak vatandaşın/üyenin sesinin duyulduğu ve alınacak kararlarda yetki sahibi olduğu bir siyaseti adım adım inşa ediyoruz/edeceğiz.”</p>

<h3><span style="color:#e74c3c;"><span style="font-size:20px;">EVCEN'DEN ÜYELERE SANDIK ÇAĞRISI</h3>

<p>YENİ Parti Karaman İl Başkanı Ahmet Recai Evcen de oyunu kullanarak parti üyelerini seçime katılmaya davet etti.</p>

<p>Evcen, yaptığı açıklamada şu sözlere yer verdi:</p>

<p><img alt="" class="image-align-left" height="200" src="https://images.karamangundem.com/uploads/2026/11/896435800-10164855679258604-4250114803004336099-n-6af04f41f2cbf.jpg" width="150" />“Yeni Parti Genel başkanlık ön seçiminde oyumuzu kullandık. Demokrasinin ve üyelerimizin iradesinin en güçlü şekilde yansıdığı bu önemli seçimde tüm üyelerimizi oy kullanmaya davet ediyoruz. Demokrasiye, değişime ve geleceğimize birlikte sahip çıkalım!”</p>

<p><span style="color:#e74c3c;"><strong>GENEL BAŞKANI BELİRLEME SÜRECİNDE ÜYELER SÖZ SAHİBİ</strong></span></p>

<p>YENİ Parti'nin 14-15 Kasım tarihlerinde Ankara'da gerçekleştireceği 1. Olağan Kurultay öncesinde düzenlenen ön seçimde, 5 Kasım'a kadar partiye üye olanlara oy kullanma hakkı tanındı.</p>

<p>İlçe ve il başkanlıkları için yapılan ön seçimlerin ardından genel başkanlık sürecinde de üyelerin görüşlerine başvuruluyor. </p>

<p><span style="color:#e74c3c;"><strong>ÖZGÜR ÖZEL'DEN 250 BİN KİŞİLİK KAMPANYA PLANI</strong></span></p>

<p>YENİ Parti Genel Başkanı Özgür Özel de Ankara İl Başkanlığında oyunu kullandı.</p>

<p>Özel, ön seçim kapsamında yaptığı açıklamada gelecek seçimler için 250 bin kişilik bir saha örgütlenmesi oluşturmayı hedeflediklerini belirtti. Parti üyelerinin yanı sıra gönüllülerin de katılabileceği çalışmalarla seçmenlere doğrudan ulaşmayı planladıklarını söyledi. </p>

<p>YENİ Parti'de genel başkanlık ön seçiminin ardından gözler 14-15 Kasım tarihlerinde Ankara'da gerçekleştirilecek olağan kurultaya çevrile<img alt="" height="360" src="https://images.karamangundem.com/uploads/2026/11/889001903-18651202462041492-2449856035697751594-n-6af04f5c028d2.jpg" width="640" /><img alt="" height="360" src="https://images.karamangundem.com/uploads/2026/11/893978783-10164855679353604-2986595758620195392-n-6af04f5c22b4d.jpg" width="640" />cek.</p></div>

                                                                            <div data-tmv-module="emoji" x-data="emojiComponent(471998, [{&quot;id&quot;:1,&quot;name&quot;:&quot;Be\u011fendim&quot;,&quot;icon&quot;:&quot;win.svg&quot;,&quot;count&quot;:0,&quot;selected&quot;:false},{&quot;id&quot;:2,&quot;name&quot;:&quot;Alk\u0131\u015f&quot;,&quot;icon&quot;:&quot;alkis.svg&quot;,&quot;count&quot;:0,&quot;selected&quot;:false},{&quot;id&quot;:5,&quot;name&quot;:&quot;K\u0131zg\u0131n&quot;,&quot;icon&quot;:&quot;kizgin.svg&quot;,&quot;count&quot;:0,&quot;selected&quot;:false},{&quot;id&quot;:6,&quot;name&quot;:&quot;Hahaha&quot;,&quot;icon&quot;:&quot;hahaha.svg&quot;,&quot;count&quot;:0,&quot;selected&quot;:false},{&quot;id&quot;:7,&quot;name&quot;:&quot;\u0130nan\u0131lmaz&quot;,&quot;icon&quot;:&quot;inanilmaz.svg&quot;,&quot;count&quot;:0,&quot;selected&quot;:false},{&quot;id&quot;:8,&quot;name&quot;:&quot;\u00dczg\u00fcn&quot;,&quot;icon&quot;:&quot;uzgun.svg&quot;,&quot;count&quot;:0,&quot;selected&quot;:false}])" class="rounded-tm flex flex-nowrap

    <template x-for="emoji in emojis" :key="emoji.id">
        
        <div :class="{'bg-[rgba(136,141,150,0.16)]': emoji.selected}"
             class="px-2 md:px-4 relative min-w-[60px] md:flex-1 rounded-xl transition-colors
                    hover:bg-[rgba(136,141,150,0.14)]
                    md:bg-[rgba(136,141,150,0.07)] md:dark:bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.05)] md:pt-[0.6rem] md:pb-[0.7rem] md:mx-1">
            <button @click="selectEmoji(emoji)"
                    @mouseenter="hovered = emoji.id" @mouseleave="hovered = null"
                    :disabled="emoji.loading"
                    :class="{'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed': emoji.loading}"
                    
                    class="px-2 md:px-2 md:w-full emotion flex flex-col items-center
                    space-y-2 p-2 md:space-y-1 md:py-1
                    rounded
                    transition-all">

                <img x-show="!emoji.loading"
                     :src="'/build/images/emojis/' + emoji.icon"
                     :alt="emoji.name"
                     class="emotion-img w-10 h-10 md:w-9 md:h-[31px] hover:scale-110 transition-transform">

                <!-- Loading spinner -->
                <svg x-show="emoji.loading"
                     class="w-6 h-6 md:w-5 md:h-5 animate-spin text-gray-500"
                     xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
                     fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">

                            stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle>

                          d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8v4l3-3-3-3v4a12 12 0 00-12 12h4z"></path>
                </svg>

                <div x-show="hovered === emoji.id"
                     class="emotion-name absolute -top-10 md:-top-2 w-auto bg-gray-700 text-white text-xs rounded px-2 py-1">
                    <span x-text="emoji.name"></span>
                </div>
                <div class="emotion-count w-full">
                    <div class="emotion-count-perc" :style="'height:' + emoji.percentage + '%'"></div>
                </div>
                <span x-text="emoji.count" class="emotion-count-text text-xs"></span>
            </button>
        </div>
    </template>

<script>
    function emojiComponent(postId, initialEmojis) {
        return {
            emojis: initialEmojis.map(e => ({
                id: e.id,
                name: e.name,
                icon: e.icon,
                count: e.count,
                selected: e.selected,
                percentage: 0,
                loading: false,
            })),
            hovered: null,

            async getFreshCsrfToken() {
                try {
                    const res = await fetch('/csrf-token.js', { cache: 'no-store' });
                    const text = await res.text();
                    const match = text.match(/'(.+)'/);
                    return match ? match[1] : null;
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error('CSRF token alınamadı:', err);
                    return null;
                }
            },

            init() {
                this.updatePercentages();
            },

            async selectEmoji(emoji) {
                if (!emoji.selected && this.emojis.filter(e => e.selected).length >= 2) {
                    Swal.fire({
                        title: "Uyar\u0131",
                        text: "En fazla iki farkl\u0131 emojiye oy verebilirsiniz.",
                        icon: 'warning',
                        confirmButtonColor: '#d33',
                        timer: 2000,
                        showConfirmButton: false
                    });
                    return;
                }

                const previousState = emoji.selected;
                const previousCount = emoji.count;
                emoji.selected = !emoji.selected;
                this.hovered = null;
                emoji.loading = true;

                try {
                    await this.sendEmojiData(emoji, previousState, previousCount);
                    this.updatePercentages();
                } catch (error) {
                    emoji.selected = previousState;
                    emoji.count = previousCount;
                    this.updatePercentages();
                    Swal.fire({
                        title: "Hata",
                        text: "Bir hata olu\u015ftu, l\u00fctfen tekrar deneyin.",
                        icon: 'error',
                        confirmButtonColor: '#d33',
                        timer: 2000,
                        showConfirmButton: false
                    });
                } finally {
                    emoji.loading = false;
                }
            },

            async sendEmojiData(emoji, previousState, previousCount) {
                const csrfToken = await this.getFreshCsrfToken();
                if (!csrfToken) {
                    throw new Error("CSRF token al\u0131namad\u0131");
                }

                const response = await fetch(`/posts/${postId}/emojis`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'X-CSRF-TOKEN': csrfToken,
                        'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        emoji_id: emoji.id,
                        post_id: postId,
                        selected: emoji.selected
                    })
                });

                const data = await response.json();

                if (!response.ok || data.error) {
                    Swal.fire({
                        title: "Uyar\u0131",
                        text: data.error || "Bir hata olu\u015ftu.",
                        icon: 'warning',
                        timer: 2000,
                        showConfirmButton: false
                    });
                    emoji.selected = previousState;
                    emoji.count = previousCount;
                    this.updatePercentages();
                    return;
                }

                if (data.success) {
                    emoji.count = data.total;
                    this.updatePercentages();
                }
            },

            updatePercentages() {
                const total = this.emojis.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + cur.count, 0);
                this.emojis.forEach(emoji => {
                    emoji.percentage = total > 0 ? (emoji.count / total) * 100 : 0;
                });
            }
        };
    }
</script>

                                         xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                        <path
                                                d="M9.40017 16H3C1.89543 16 1 15.1046 1 14V11.8957C1 11.6344 1.05118 11.3757 1.15064 11.1342L4.24501 3.61925C4.3993 3.24455 4.76447 3 5.16969 3H22C22.5523 3 23 3.44772 23 4V14C23 14.5523 22.5523 15 22 15H18.5182C18.1932 15 17.8886 15.1579 17.7012 15.4233L12.2478 23.149C12.1053 23.3508 11.8367 23.4184 11.6157 23.3078L9.80163 22.4008C8.74998 21.875 8.20687 20.6874 8.49694 19.548L9.40017 16ZM17 13.4125V5H5.83939L3 11.8957V14H9.40017C10.7049 14 11.6602 15.229 11.3384 16.4934L10.4351 20.0414C10.3771 20.2693 10.4857 20.5068 10.6961 20.612L11.3572 20.9425L16.0673 14.27C16.3172 13.9159 16.6366 13.6257 17 13.4125ZM19 13H21V5H19V13Z"></path>
                                    </svg>
                                    <span class="mr-2">Beğenme</span>
                                    <span :id="'comment-' + comment.id + '-dislikes'"
                                          class="pr-2 pl-2 text-vs tracking-letter-1px text-red-400 bg-red-200 rounded-tm"
                                          x-text="comment.unlikes"></span>
                                </a>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div x-show="replyCommentId === comment.id" class="bg-white dark:bg-customdarkgray-800 dark:text-gray-300 p-4 mt-4 flex flex-col rounded-tm">
                            <div class="flex flex-col md:flex-row space-y-4 md:space-y-0 md:space-x-4 my-2">
                                <input x-show="!userIsLoggedIn" autocomplete="off" x-model="newReply.author" type="text" placeholder="Ad / Soyad" class="dark:bg-customdarkgray-900 w-full text-sm bg-gray-100 hover:bg-white" required>
                                                                    <input x-show="!userIsLoggedIn" autocomplete="off" x-model="newReply.email" type="email" placeholder="Email"
                                           class="dark:bg-customdarkgray-900 w-full text-sm bg-gray-100 hover:bg-white" required>
                                                            </div>
                            <textarea x-model="newReply.text" x-on:input="remainingChars = 1000 - newReply.text.length" class="dark:bg-customdarkgray-900 h-24 text-sm flex-1 mb-2 rounded border p-2 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-white" maxlength="1000" placeholder="Cevabınızı yazınız..." required></textarea>
                            <p class="text-xs mb-2">Kalan Karakter: <span class="text-red-600" x-text="remainingChars"></span></p>

<div x-show="replyCommentId === comment.id" x-data="voiceComment()" class="mt-2 mb-2">
 <button type="button"
         class="bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 text-gray-800 text-sm font-medium px-3 py-2 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 shadow-sm transition-all
         duration-150"
         x-on:click="toggleRecording">
    <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" class="w-5 h-5" viewBox="0 0 24 24"
         stroke="currentColor">
        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2"
              d="M12 18v3m6-3a6 6 0 01-12 0m12-5V9a6 6 0 00-12 0v4"/>
        <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/>
    </svg>
    <span x-text="isRecording ? 'Kaydı Durdur' : 'Ses Ekle'"></span>
</button>

        <template x-if="isRecording">
            <div class="text-xs text-red-600 mt-2">
                Süre: <span x-text="formattedTime"></span> / 02:00
            </div>
        </template>

        <template x-if="audioUrl">
            <div class="mt-2 space-y-2">
                <audio :src="audioUrl" controls class="w-full"></audio>
            </div>
        </template>

        <input type="hidden" name="audio_blob" :value="$store.commentAudio.audio_blob">
    </div>

                            <div class="flex items-center mb-2">
                                <input type="checkbox" x-model="isTOSAccepted" name="reply_usertos" :id="'reply_usertos-' + comment.id" class="mr-2">
                                <label :for="'reply_usertos-' + comment.id" class="mb-0 text-xs">
                                    <a href="#" x-on:click.prevent="openTOSModal" class="underline">Yorum yazma kurallarını</a> okudum ve kabul ediyorum.
                                </label>
                            </div>




                            <button x-on:click.prevent="addReply(comment.id)" x-bind:disabled="sending || newReply.text.length < 3 || newReply.author.length < 3 || !isTOSAccepted" class="bg-red-500 hover:bg-red-600 text-white font-semibold p-2 rounded-md mt-2 text-sm self-end">
                                Gönder
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <template x-if="comment.replies && comment.replies.length > 0">
                        <div class="replies">
                            <template x-for="reply in comment.replies" :key="reply.id">
                                <div class="reply bg-white dark:bg-customdarkgray-800 dark:text-gray-300 rounded-tm p-3 space-y-4 my-4 comment-reply">
                                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                                        <div x-text="reply.name" class="font-bold text-sm"></div>
                                        <div x-text="reply.created_at" class="text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-300"></div>
                                    </div>
                                    <p x-html="reply.body" class="text-sm"></p>
                                    <template x-if="reply.audio_path">
                                        <audio :src="reply.audio_path" controls class="mt-2 w-full"></audio>
                                    </template>

                                    <div class="flex items-center space-x-4">
                                        <div class="flex items-center space-x-4">
                                            <a href="#"
                                               class="like-button text-xs hover:text-green-600 flex items-center"
                                               :data-comment-id="reply.id"
                                               @click.prevent="likeComment(reply)">
                                                <svg class="fill-current hover:text-green-600 mr-1" width="16"
                                                     height="16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
                                                     viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                                    <path
                                                            d="M14.5998 8.00033H21C22.1046 8.00033 23 8.89576 23 10.0003V12.1047C23 12.3659 22.9488 12.6246 22.8494 12.8662L19.755 20.3811C19.6007 20.7558 19.2355 21.0003 18.8303 21.0003H2C1.44772 21.0003 1 20.5526 1 20.0003V10.0003C1 9.44804 1.44772 9.00033 2 9.00033H5.48184C5.80677 9.00033 6.11143 8.84246 6.29881 8.57701L11.7522 0.851355C11.8947 0.649486 12.1633 0.581978 12.3843 0.692483L14.1984 1.59951C15.25 2.12534 15.7931 3.31292 15.5031 4.45235L14.5998 8.00033ZM7 10.5878V19.0003H18.1606L21 12.1047V10.0003H14.5998C13.2951 10.0003 12.3398 8.77128 12.6616 7.50691L13.5649 3.95894C13.6229 3.73105 13.5143 3.49353 13.3039 3.38837L12.6428 3.0578L7.93275 9.73038C7.68285 10.0844 7.36341 10.3746 7 10.5878ZM5 11.0003H3V19.0003H5V11.0003Z"></path>
                                                </svg>
                                                <span class="mr-2">Beğen</span> <span
                                                        :id="'reply-' + reply.id + '-likes'"
                                                        class="pr-2 pl-2 text-vs tracking-letter-1px text-white bg-green-400 rounded-tm"
                                                        x-text="reply.likes"></span>

                                               @click.prevent="dislikeComment(reply)">
                                                <svg class="fill-current mr-1" width="16" height="16"
                                                     xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                                                    <path

                                                </svg>
                                                <span class="mr-2">Beğenme</span>
                                                <span :id="'reply-' + reply.id + '-dislikes'"

<script>
     (function () {
         var kaydet = function () {
             if (!Alpine.store('commentAudio')) {
                 Alpine.store('commentAudio', { audio_blob: null });
             }
         };
         // Sonsuz kaydirmada Alpine zaten basladigi icin alpine:init bir daha tetiklenmez: hemen kaydet
         if (window.Alpine && typeof window.Alpine.store === 'function') {
             kaydet();
         } else {
             document.addEventListener('alpine:init', kaydet);
         }
     })();
    var loggedInUser = {"name":"","lastname":"","email":""};

    function commentHandler(postId) {
        const routes = {
            commentStore: "https://www.karamangundem.com/commentStore",
            commentShow: "https://www.karamangundem.com/post/471998/comments",
            commentReply: "https://www.karamangundem.com/commentReply",
        };

        let userIsLoggedIn = false;

            return {
            userIsLoggedIn: userIsLoggedIn,
            postId: postId,
            comments: [],
            sending: false,
            newComment: {
                text: '',
                email: loggedInUser.email,
                author: loggedInUser.name + loggedInUser.lastname,
                postId: postId,
                parent_id: 0,
                post_type: 1
            },
            newReply: {
                text: '',
                email: loggedInUser.email,
                author: loggedInUser.name + loggedInUser.lastname,
                postId: postId,
                post_type: 1,
                depth: 1
            },
            replyCommentId: null,
            remainingChars: 1000,
            isTOSAccepted: false,
            isTOSModalOpen: false,
            showMainCommentForm: true,

            openTOSModal() {
                this.isTOSModalOpen = true;
            },

            acceptTOS() {
                this.isTOSAccepted = true;
                this.isTOSModalOpen = false;
            },

            toggleReplyForm(commentId, currentDepth) {
                this.replyCommentId = this.replyCommentId === commentId ? null : commentId;
                this.newReply.depth = (currentDepth + 1) || 1;

                this.showMainCommentForm = this.replyCommentId === null;
            },

            async fetchComments() {
                try {
                    this.comments = [];
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error("Comments could not be fetched: ", error.message);
                }
            },

            async addComment() {
                // Cift tiklama / iki kez Enter: istek ~1 sn surerken ikinci gonderim moderasyona ayni yorumu dusuruyordu
                if (this.sending) return;
                const commentData = {
                    text: this.newComment.text,
                    email: this.newComment.email,
                    author: this.newComment.author,
                    postId: this.newComment.postId,
                    parent_id: this.newComment.parent_id,
                    post_type: this.newComment.post_type,
                    audio_blob: Alpine.store('commentAudio').audio_blob || null,
                };

                if (!this.isTOSAccepted) {
                    Swal.fire({ title: "Hata", text: "Kurallar\u0131 kabul etmelisiniz", icon: 'error', timer: 3000, showConfirmButton: false });
                    return;
                }

                if (!commentData.text || commentData.text.length < 3) {
                    Swal.fire({ title: "Hata", text: "Yorum en az 3 karakter olmal\u0131", icon: 'warning', timer: 3000, showConfirmButton: false });
                    return;
                }

                // Kilit CSRF isteginden ONCE: o ~200 ms'de ikinci tik de iki POST gonderiyordu
                this.sending = true;
                let csrfToken = null;
                try {
                    const res = await fetch('/csrf-token.js', { cache: 'no-store' });
                    const text = await res.text();
                    const match = text.match(/'(.+)'/);
                    csrfToken = match ? match[1] : null;
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error('CSRF token alınamadı:', err);
                    Swal.fire({ title: "Hata", text: "CSRF token al\u0131namad\u0131", icon: 'error', timer: 3000, showConfirmButton: false });
                    this.sending = false;
                    return;
                }

                try {
                    
                    let response = await fetch(routes.commentStore, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: {
                            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': csrfToken,
                            'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'
                        },
                        body: JSON.stringify(commentData)
                    });

                    let data = await response.json();

                    if (response.ok) {
                        Swal.fire({ title: "Ba\u015far\u0131l\u0131!", text: "Yorum g\u00f6nderildi", icon: 'success', timer: 3000, showConfirmButton: false });
                        this.handleNewComment(data);
                        this.showCommentForm = false;
                    } else {
                        Swal.fire({ title: "Hata!", text: data.message || "Bir hata olu\u015ftu", icon: 'error', timer: 3000, showConfirmButton: false });
                    }
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error('Yorum gönderilemedi:', error);
                    Swal.fire({ title: "Hata!", text: "Yorum g\u00f6nderilirken hata olu\u015ftu", icon: 'error', timer: 3000, showConfirmButton: false });
                } finally {
                    this.sending = false;
                }
            },

            checkLoginBeforeReply(commentId, depth) {
                fetch('/api/user-status')
                    .then(response => response.json())
                    .then(data => {
                        const commentsSecurity = data.commentsSecurity;
                        this.userIsLoggedIn = data.loggedIn;

                        if (this.userIsLoggedIn) {
                            this.newReply.author = data.user.name + ' ' + data.user.lastname;
                            this.newReply.email = data.user.email;
                            this.toggleReplyForm(commentId, depth);
                        } else {
                            if (commentsSecurity == 0) {
                                this.toggleReplyForm(commentId, depth);
                            } else {
                                Swal.fire({
                                    title: "Uyar\u0131",
                                    text: "Cevap yazabilmek i\u00e7in giri\u015f yapman\u0131z gerekmektedir.",
                                    icon: 'warning',
                                    showCancelButton: true,
                                    confirmButtonText: "\u00dcye Giri\u015fi Yap",
                                    cancelButtonText: "Kapat",
                                    reverseButtons: true
                                }).then((result) => {
                                    if (result.isConfirmed) {
                                        window.location.href = "https://www.karamangundem.com/login";
                                    }
                                });
                            }
                        }
                    })
                    .catch(error => {
                        console.error('API Error:', error);
                    });
            },

            checkLoginStatus() {

                fetch('/api/user-status', { headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json', 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' } })
                    .then(response => {
                        if (!response.ok) { throw new Error('user-status ' + response.status); }
                        return response.json();
                    })
                    .then(data => {
                        const commentsSecurity = data.commentsSecurity;
                        this.userIsLoggedIn = data.loggedIn;

                        if (this.userIsLoggedIn) {
                            this.newComment.author = data.user.name + ' ' + data.user.lastname;
                            this.newComment.email = data.user.email;
                            this.showCommentForm = true;
                        } else {

                            if (commentsSecurity == 0) {
                                this.showCommentForm = true;
                            } else {
                                this.showCommentForm = false;

                                Swal.fire({
                                    title: "Uyar\u0131",
                                    text: "Yorum yapabilmek i\u00e7in giri\u015f yapman\u0131z gerekmektedir.",
                                    icon: 'warning',
                                    showCancelButton: true,
                                    confirmButtonText: "\u00dcye Giri\u015fi Yap",
                                    cancelButtonText: "Kapat",
                                    reverseButtons: true
                                }).then((result) => {
                                    if (result.isConfirmed) {
                                        window.location.href = "https://www.karamangundem.com/login";
                                    }
                                });

                            }
                        }
                    })
                    .catch(() => {
                        this.showCommentForm = false;
                        Swal.fire({ title: "Hata!", text: "Bir hata olu\u015ftu.", icon: 'error', timer: 2500, showConfirmButton: false });
                    });
            },

            handleNewComment(comment) {
                if (comment.status === 1) {
                    this.comments.push(comment);
                }
                // Ad/e-posta korunur: giris yapmis okurda ad alani gizli; '' yazilinca ikinci yorumda Gonder kalici kilitleniyordu
                this.newComment = {
                    text: '',
                    author: this.newComment.author,
                    email: this.newComment.email,
                    postId: this.postId,
                    parent_id: 0,
                    post_type: 1
                };
                this.isTOSAccepted = false;
                this.sesiSifirla();
            },

            sesiSifirla() {
                Alpine.store('commentAudio').audio_blob = null;
                document.querySelectorAll('[x-data^="voiceComment"]').forEach(el => {
                    const d = el._x_dataStack?.[0];
                    if (d) d.audioUrl = null;
                });
            },

            isReply(comment) {
                return comment.parent_id !== 0;
            },

            async addReply(parentCommentId) {
                if (this.sending) return;
                if (!this.isTOSAccepted) {
                    Swal.fire({ title: 'Hata!', text: "L\u00fctfen hizmet \u015fartlar\u0131n\u0131 kabul edin.", icon: 'error', timer: 3000, showConfirmButton: false });
                    return;
                }

                this.newReply.parent_id = parentCommentId;

                this.sending = true;
                let csrfToken = null;
                try {
                    const res = await fetch('/csrf-token.js', { cache: 'no-store' });
                    const text = await res.text();
                    const match = text.match(/'(.+)'/);
                    csrfToken = match ? match[1] : null;
                } catch (err) {
                    Swal.fire({ title: "Hata", text: "CSRF token al\u0131namad\u0131", icon: 'error', timer: 3000, showConfirmButton: false });
                    this.sending = false;
                    return;
                }

                const voiceComponent = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[x-data^="voiceComment"]'))
                    .map(el => el._x_dataStack?.[0]).find(d => d?.isRecording);
                if (voiceComponent?.isRecording) {
                    voiceComponent.stopRecording();
                    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 1500));
                }

                this.newReply.audio_blob = Alpine.store('commentAudio').audio_blob || null;

                try {
                    
                    let response = await fetch(routes.commentReply, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: {
                            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': csrfToken,
                            'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'
                        },
                        body: JSON.stringify(this.newReply)
                    });

                    let data = await response.json();
                    if (response.ok) {
                        Swal.fire({ title: 'Başarılı!', text: 'Yorumunuz gönderildi', icon: 'success', timer: 3000, showConfirmButton: false });
                        this.handleNewReply(data);
                    } else {
                        Swal.fire({ title: 'Hata!', text: data.message || "Hata Olu\u015ftu", icon: 'error', timer: 3000, showConfirmButton: false });
                    }
                } catch (error) {
                    Swal.fire({ title: 'Hata!', text: error.message || "Hata Olu\u015ftu", icon: 'error', timer: 3000, showConfirmButton: false });
                } finally {
                    this.sending = false;
                }
            },

            handleNewReply(reply) {
                let parentComment = this.comments.find(comment => comment.id === reply.parent_id);
                // Ana yorum gibi: onay bekleyen cevap yayinda sanilmasin (sunucu cevaplari status=0 kaydeder)
                if (parentComment && reply.status === 1) {
                    if (!parentComment.replies) {
                        parentComment.replies = [];
                    }
                    parentComment.replies.push(reply);
                }
                this.newReply = {
                    text: '',
                    author: this.newReply.author,
                    email: this.newReply.email,
                    postId: this.postId,
                    post_type: 1,
                    depth: 1,
                    audio_blob: null
                };
                this.replyCommentId = null;
                this.showMainCommentForm = true;
                this.isTOSAccepted = false;
                this.sesiSifirla();
            },

            likeComment(comment) {
                this.updateVote(comment, 'like');
            },

            dislikeComment(comment) {
                this.updateVote(comment, 'dislike');
            },


            async updateVote(comment, action) {
                // 🔑 TAZE CSRF token — sayfa Cloudflare/ResponseCache ile cache'lenebildiğinden meta
                // tag'deki token bayat kalabilir; diğer yorum fonksiyonları (gönder/yanıtla/sesli) gibi
                // /csrf-token.js'ten TAZE çekiyoruz. Önceden burada `this.csrfToken` (bileşende TANIMSIZ)
                // vardı → oy isteği 'X-CSRF-TOKEN: undefined' gönderip 419 (CSRF mismatch) alıyordu.
                let csrfToken = null;
                try {
                    const res = await fetch('/csrf-token.js', { cache: 'no-store' });
                    const text = await res.text();
                    const match = text.match(/'(.+)'/);
                    csrfToken = match ? match[1] : null;
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error('Oy için CSRF token alınamadı:', err);
                    return;
                }

                fetch(`/comments/${comment.id}/${action}`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'X-CSRF-TOKEN': csrfToken,
                        'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
                        'Accept': 'application/json'
                    }
                })
                    .then(response => {
                        if (!response.ok) throw response;
                        return response.json();
                    })
                    .then(data => {
                        if (data.success === false && data.error) {
                            Swal.fire({
                                title: "Hata!",
                                text: data.error,
                                icon: 'error',
                                timer: 3000,
                                showConfirmButton: false
                            });
                        } else {
                            comment.likes = data.likes;
                            comment.unlikes = data.unlikes;
                        }
                    })
                    .catch(error => {
                        if (error.json) {
                            error.json().then(err => {
                                Swal.fire({
                                    title: "Hata!",
                                    text: err.error || "Bir hata olu\u015ftu!",
                                    icon: 'error',
                                    timer: 3000,
                                    showConfirmButton: false
                                });
                            });
                        } else {
                            console.error('Error:', error);
                        }
                    });
            },
        }
    }

     function voiceComment() {
         return {
             isRecording: false,
             mediaRecorder: null,
             audioChunks: [],
             audioUrl: null,
             timer: null,
             seconds: 0,
             maxSeconds: 120,

             get formattedTime() {
                 const min = String(Math.floor(this.seconds / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
                 const sec = String(this.seconds % 60).padStart(2, '0');
                 return `${min}:${sec}`;
             },


             async toggleRecording() {
                 if (this.isRecording) {
                     this.stopRecording();
                 } else {
                     this.audioChunks = [];
                     this.audioUrl = null;
                     Alpine.store('commentAudio').audio_blob = null;

                     // Izin reddi / http sayfa / uygulama ici tarayici (mediaDevices yok) / eski iOS (MediaRecorder yok)
                     try {
                         if (!navigator.mediaDevices || !navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia || !window.MediaRecorder) {
                             throw new Error('unsupported');
                         }
                         const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
                             audio: true
                         });
                         const options = MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported('audio/webm;codecs=opus')
                             ? { mimeType: 'audio/webm;codecs=opus' }
                             : {};
                         this.mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream, options);
                     } catch (err) {
                         Swal.fire({
                             title: "Hata!",
                             text: "Mikrofon izni verilmedi ya da taray\u0131c\u0131n\u0131z ses kayd\u0131n\u0131 desteklemiyor.",
                             icon: 'error'
                         });
                         return;
                     }

                     this.mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (e) => {
                         if (e.data && e.data.size > 0) {
                             this.audioChunks.push(e.data);
                         }
                     };

                     this.mediaRecorder.onstop = () => {
                         const blob = new Blob(this.audioChunks, { type: 'audio/webm' });

                         if (blob.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
                             Swal.fire({
                                 title: "Hata!",
                                 text: "Ses kayd\u0131 5MB \u00fczeri olamaz.",
                                 icon: 'error',
                                 timer: 4000,
                                 showConfirmButton: false
                             });
                             return;
                         }

                         this.audioUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

                         const reader = new FileReader();
                         reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
                         reader.onloadend = () => {
                             const base64 = reader.result;
                             Alpine.store('commentAudio').audio_blob = base64;
                         };

                         this.audioChunks = [];
                         this.seconds = 0;
                         clearInterval(this.timer);
                     };

                     this.mediaRecorder.start(250);
                     this.isRecording = true;
                     this.timer = setInterval(() => {
                         this.seconds++;
                         if (this.seconds >= this.maxSeconds) {
                             this.stopRecording();
                         }
                     }, 1000);
                 }
             },

             stopRecording() {

                 if (this.mediaRecorder && this.mediaRecorder.state !== 'inactive') {
                     this.mediaRecorder.stop();
                 }

                 if (this.mediaRecorder && this.mediaRecorder.stream) {
                     this.mediaRecorder.stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
                 }

                 this.isRecording = false;
                 clearInterval(this.timer);
             }
         };
     }
</script>

    function scrollUp() {
        const container = document.querySelector('[x-ref="authorsContainer"]');
        container.scrollBy({
            top: -100,
            behavior: 'smooth'
        });
    }

    function scrollDown() {
        const container = document.querySelector('[x-ref="authorsContainer"]');
        container.scrollBy({
            top: 100,
            behavior: 'smooth'
        });
    }

    function handleScroll(event) {
        const container = document.querySelector('[x-ref="authorsContainer"]');
        container.scrollTop += event.deltaY;
    }
</script>

    <script>
function importantPost() {
    return {
        showPost: false,
        post: [],
        panelTitle: "G\u00fcn\u00fcn Geli\u015fmeleri",
        loadPost() {
            fetch("https://www.karamangundem.com/important-post", { headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest', 'Accept': 'application/json' }})
                .then(r => { if (!r.ok) { throw new Error('important ' + r.status); } return r.json(); })
                .then(data => {

                    if (Array.isArray(data)) {
                        this.post = data;
                        this.panelTitle = data.length ? "G\u00fcn\u00fcn Geli\u015fmeleri" : "\u0130lginizi \u00c7ekebilir";
                        if (!data.length) this.fetchYesterdayFallback();
                    } else {
                        this.post = data.items || [];
                        this.panelTitle = data.title || (this.post.length ? "G\u00fcn\u00fcn Geli\u015fmeleri" : "\u0130lginizi \u00c7ekebilir");
                    }
                })
                .catch(err => console.error('Önemli gönderi yüklenirken hata:', err));
        },

        fetchYesterdayFallback() {  },

        togglePostPanel() {
            this.showPost = !this.showPost;
            if (this.showPost && !this.post.length) this.loadPost();
        },
        closePostPanel() { this.showPost = false; },
        setCookieAndClose() {
            document.cookie = "hidePostPanel=true; max-age=3600; path=/";
            document.querySelector('[x-data="importantPost()"]').style.display = 'none';
        }
    }
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    if (document.cookie.split(';').some((c) => c.trim().startsWith('hidePostPanel='))) {
        const el = document.querySelector('[x-data="importantPost()"]');
        if (el) el.style.display = 'none';
    }
});
</script>

<script>
    function ccConsent() {
        const STORAGE_KEY = 'cc_consent_v2';
        const VERSION = 2;
        const MAX_AGE_MS = 180 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

        return {
            visible: false,
            showDetails: false,
            expanded: false,
            prefs: { functionality: false, ads: false, personalization: false },

            init() {
                try {
                    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
                    if (!raw) { this.visible = true; return; }
                    const data = JSON.parse(raw);
                    if (data.version !== VERSION) { this.visible = true; return; }
                    if (!data.ts || (Date.now() - data.ts) > MAX_AGE_MS) { this.visible = true; return; }
                    this.applyToGtag({
                        functionality:   !!data.functionality,
                        ads:             !!data.ads,
                        personalization: !!data.personalization,
                    });
                } catch (e) { this.visible = true; }
            },

            acceptAll() { this.persist({ functionality: true, ads: true, personalization: true }); },
            rejectAll() { this.persist({ functionality: false, ads: false, personalization: false }); },
            saveCustom() {
                this.persist({
                    functionality:   !!this.prefs.functionality,
                    ads:             !!this.prefs.ads,
                    personalization: !!this.prefs.personalization,
                });
            },

            persist(p) {
                try {
                    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ version: VERSION, ts: Date.now(), ...p }));
                } catch (e) {}
                this.applyToGtag(p);
                this.visible = false;
            },

            applyToGtag(p) {
                if (typeof window.gtag !== 'function') {
                    window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                    window.gtag = function () { window.dataLayer.push(arguments); };
                }
                window.gtag('consent', 'update', {
                    'functionality_storage':   p.functionality   ? 'granted' : 'denied',
                    'personalization_storage': p.personalization ? 'granted' : 'denied',
                    'ad_storage':              p.ads             ? 'granted' : 'denied',
                    'ad_user_data':            p.ads             ? 'granted' : 'denied',
                    'ad_personalization':      p.personalization ? 'granted' : 'denied',
                });
            },
        };
    }

    window.resetCookieConsent = function () {
        try { localStorage.removeItem('cc_consent_v2'); } catch (e) {}
        location.reload();
    };
</script>

    <script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
    if (!sidebar) return;

    let lastKnownScrollY = 0;
    let ticking = false;

    const handleScroll = () => {
        if (lastKnownScrollY > 100) {
            sidebar.classList.add('sticky', 'top-24');
        } else {
            sidebar.classList.remove('sticky', 'top-24');
        }
        ticking = false;
    };

    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        lastKnownScrollY = window.scrollY;
        if (!ticking) {
            window.requestAnimationFrame(handleScroll);
            ticking = true;
        }
    });
});
</script>

                                  || featIds.indexOf(String(p.type)) !== -1
                                  || feats.indexOf(p.type) !== -1
                                  || feats.indexOf(String(p.type)) !== -1;
                     if (! canAccess) {
                         var msg = data.isLoggedIn ? "Bu i\u00e7erik i\u00e7in aboneli\u011finiz yetersiz." : "Bu i\u00e7erik sadece abonelere \u00f6zeldir. L\u00fctfen giri\u015f yap\u0131n.";
                         var modal = document.querySelector('.error-modal');
                         var msgEl = document.querySelector('.error-modal-message');
                         if (modal) {
                             if (msgEl) msgEl.innerText = msg;
                             modal.style.display = 'flex';
                             modal.classList.remove('hidden');
                             document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                         }
                     }
                 }

                 var premiumIds = Array.isArray(data.accessibleFeatureIds) ? data.accessibleFeatureIds.map(String) : [];
                 var fillPremium = function () {
                     Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.tmv-premium-body:not([data-done])'), function (box) {
                         if (premiumIds.indexOf(String(box.getAttribute('data-type'))) === -1) return;
                         box.setAttribute('data-done', '1');
                         fetch('/api/post-body/' + parseInt(box.getAttribute('data-post'), 10), { cache: 'no-store', credentials: 'same-origin' })
                             .then(function (r) {
                                 if (r.ok) return r.json();
                                 if (r.status === 429 || r.status >= 500) throw new Error('retry');
                                 return null;
                             })
                             .then(function (j) {
                                 if (! j || typeof j.html !== 'string') return;
                                 box.innerHTML = j.html;
                                 Array.prototype.forEach.call(box.querySelectorAll('script'), function (o) {
                                     var n = document.createElement('script');
                                     for (var i = 0; i < o.attributes.length; i++) n.setAttribute(o.attributes[i].name, o.attributes[i].value);
                                     n.text = o.text;
                                     o.parentNode.replaceChild(n, o);
                                 });
                                 [['blockquote.twitter-tweet', 'https://platform.twitter.com/widgets.js'], ['blockquote.instagram-media', 'https://www.instagram.com/embed.js'], ['blockquote.tiktok-embed', 'https://www.tiktok.com/embed.js']].forEach(function (e) {
                                     if (! box.querySelector(e[0]) || document.querySelector('script[src="' + e[1] + '"]')) return;
                                     var s = document.createElement('script');
                                     s.async = true;
                                     s.src = e[1];
                                     document.body.appendChild(s);
                                 });
                                 if (window.twttr && twttr.widgets && twttr.widgets.load) twttr.widgets.load(box);
                                 if (window.instgrm && instgrm.Embeds && instgrm.Embeds.process) instgrm.Embeds.process();
                             })
                             .catch(function () {
                                 var tries = parseInt(box.getAttribute('data-try') || '0', 10);
                                 if (tries >= 2) return;
                                 box.setAttribute('data-try', String(tries + 1));
                                 setTimeout(function () { box.removeAttribute('data-done'); fillPremium(); }, 4000 * (tries + 1));
                             });
                     });
                 };
                 if (subEnabled && premiumIds.length) {
                     fillPremium();
                     var premiumQueued = false;
                     if (window.MutationObserver) new MutationObserver(function () {
                         if (premiumQueued) return;
                         premiumQueued = true;
                         requestAnimationFrame(function () { premiumQueued = false; fillPremium(); });
                     }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
                 }

                 var feDesktop = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(min-width: 1024px) and (any-pointer: fine)').matches;
                 var feWanted = feDesktop ? data.canFrontEdit : (data.canFrontAds && false);
                 var feSrc = "\/admin\/front-editor\/script";
                 if (feSrc && feWanted && window.top === window && ! document.getElementById('tmv-front-editor')) {
                     var fe = document.createElement('script');
                     fe.id = 'tmv-front-editor';
                     fe.src = feSrc + '?v=2898&l=tr';
                     document.body.appendChild(fe);
                 }
             });

             if (typeof showUserMenu === 'function') {
                 showUserMenu("https://www.karamangundem.com/profile", "https://www.karamangundem.com/logout", "https://www.karamangundem.com/login");
             }
         });
      </script>

      <script>
         (function() {
             function loadOnce(src) {
                 if (document.querySelector('script[src="' + src + '"]')) return;
                 var s = document.createElement('script');
                 s.async = true;
                 s.src = src;
                 s.charset = 'utf-8';
                 document.body.appendChild(s);
             }
             if (document.querySelector('blockquote.twitter-tweet')) {
                 loadOnce('https://platform.twitter.com/widgets.js');
             }
             if (document.querySelector('blockquote.instagram-media')) {
                 loadOnce('https://www.instagram.com/embed.js');
             }
             if (document.querySelector('blockquote.tiktok-embed')) {
                 loadOnce('https://www.tiktok.com/embed.js');
             }
         })();
      </script>
                  <script>
         (function () {
            var queued = false;
            function fitTall() {
               queued = false;
               Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.post-body iframe[width][height]:not([data-tall])'), function (f) {
                  var ws = f.getAttribute('width'), hs = f.getAttribute('height');
                  if (!/^\d+(px)?$/.test(ws) || !/^\d+(px)?$/.test(hs) || /youtu/.test(f.src || '')) return;
                  var w = parseFloat(ws), h = parseFloat(hs);
                  if (!(w > 0 && h > w * 1.15)) return;
                  f.setAttribute('data-tall', '1');
                  f.style.width = w + 'px';
                  f.style.maxWidth = '100%';
                  f.style.height = 'auto';
                  f.style.aspectRatio = w + ' / ' + h;
                  f.style.marginInline = 'auto';
               });
            }
            fitTall();
            if (window.MutationObserver) new MutationObserver(function () {
               if (queued) return;
               queued = true;
               requestAnimationFrame(fitTall);
            }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
         })();
      </script>
